## Change Log

### 7.0.0 (Fri Oct 16 12:30:36 2026 -0700)

**Database Migrations**

- [#43939](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/43939) fix(migrations): add functional index on ab_user(lower(username)) for case-insensitive login (@eschutho)
- [#43407](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/43407) feat: migrate Global Async Queries onto the Global Task Framework (@villebro)
- [#43783](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/43783) fix(semantic-layers): classify SemanticLayer/SemanticView in FAB role sets (@mikebridge)
- [#43724](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/43724) feat(dataset): warn about dependent charts and dashboards before purging an archived dataset (@mikebridge)
- [#43485](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/43485) feat(deletion-retention): persist purge block reason codes on the audit log (@mikebridge)
- [#43273](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/43273) chore: Unset future flag in engines and sessions. (@hy144328)
- [#42897](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42897) fix(models): enforce one OAuth2 token per user+database (@rusackas)
- [#42803](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42803) feat: bump SQLAlchemy to 2.0 and flask-sqlalchemy to 3.1.1 (@rusackas)
- [#42888](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42888) refactor(deletion-retention): add declarative purge policies (@mikebridge)
- [#42494](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42494) feat(alerts-reports): add per-schedule toggle to include/exclude the Explore in Superset link (@bxsx)
- [#42863](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42863) fix(deletion-retention): dedupe repeated blocked audits (@mikebridge)
- [#42878](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42878) fix(migrations): merge divergent Alembic heads into a single chain (@sadpandajoe)
- [#41714](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/41714) chore(viz): remove legacy explore_json + viz.py pipeline (@rusackas)
- [#28627](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/28627) fix(engine): update databend engine spec for dialect version >=0.4.6 (@rad-pat)

- [#42365](https://github.com/apache/superset/pull/42365) chore: session enforce sqlalchemy 2.0 (@hy144328)
